ChatGPT Desktop 26. release notes, July 23, 2026 (Codex-tagged entry in the official ChatGPT changelog). Multi-folder local projects: “Local projects in the ChatGPT desktop app can now include multiple related folders. From a project’s menu, select Edit project to add folders and choose the primary folder.” Discovery semantics, verbatim: “New chats, Git operations, and automatic discovery of AGENTS.md, skills, and config.toml use the primary folder. Secondary folders remain available for file search, reading, and editing.” ChatGPT Voice: “Powered by GPT-Live, ChatGPT Voice lets you talk through work and coordinate tasks in Chat, Work, and Codex in the ChatGPT desktop app.” Availability is Plus, Pro, Business, Edu, and Enterprise, on desktop and through Remote on iOS; on macOS you can “turn on Screen context to share an appshot of your frontmost window.” Both are desktop-app changes; neither alters CLI behavior. ↩↩↩↩

ChatGPT for iOS 1.2026. release notes, July 20, 2026 (Codex-tagged entry in the official ChatGPT changelog): “Added support for interactive forms in Codex tasks”; “Added support for rendering Mermaid diagrams inline in task transcripts”; recovery of unsent prompts across tasks, hosts, and workspaces; goals that resume blocked or usage-limited runs; task lists sorted by recent activity with unavailable hosts surfaced at creation time; and improved Remote onboarding, composer guidance, and iPad navigation. Recorded for completeness — this guide documents Codex’s five surfaces (CLI, desktop, IDE, cloud, Chrome) and does not cover the iOS app. ↩

Codex CLI v0.. release notes and Codex Changelog (OpenAI Developers). Promoted to stable June 9, 2026 (20:13 UTC); marked Latest on GitHub; install [email protected]`npm install -g @openai/`. New features: code mode can call standalone web search directly (including from nested JavaScript tool calls) and receive plaintext results; tool/connector input schemas preserve`oneOf`/`allOf` constructs for better large-schema structure preservation and MCP compatibility;`codex doctor` adds editor and pager environment details, redacting sensitive values in JSON; plugin marketplace automation exposes sources in`codex plugin marketplace list --json` plus faster cached-catalog plugin listing. Bug fixes:`codex resume --last`/`codex fork --last` treat trailing args as prompts rather than session IDs; subagent MCP startup warnings no longer surface in the wrong thread context; image edits reference exact file paths; tilde URLs linkify fully in the TUI; thread resets (`/new`,`/clear`,`/fork`) preserve cloud-managed requirements/feature flags; sandbox execution consistently preserves approval decisions and enforces proxy-only networking. Maintenance: separate symbol archives with line tables;`rusty_v8` bumped to 149... The latest pre-release as of June 9, 2026 is the`rust-v0..-alpha` line (prerelease) — 0.. is still in alpha and is not tracked by this guide, which follows stable. Current-session verification June 9, 2026: GitHub releases and the OpenAI Codex changelog confirm`rust-v0..` as the latest stable. ↩↩↩

Codex CLI v0.. release notes and Codex Changelog (OpenAI Developers). Promoted to stable June 8, 2026 (23:00 UTC);`prerelease: false`, marked Latest on GitHub; install [email protected]`npm install -g @openai/`. Adds`/app` desktop handoff on macOS and Windows, local image paths exposed to models, more flexible reasoning-effort selection, and structured JSON output for plugin automation. Codex CLI v0.. release notes and Codex Changelog (OpenAI Developers). Promoted to stable June 4, 2026 (01:17 UTC) from the v0..-alpha line. TUI: F13–F keybindings, paste in searchable menus, compact reasoning-only status/title item. Enterprise: monthly credit limits in admin flows and cloud-managed config bundles. Remote control: client-initiated pairing and controller-grant management. Plugins: machine-readable JSON output for plugin workflows and cached remote catalog suggestions. Hosted web/image tools in more code-mode flows with standalone web searches running in parallel; multi-agent v2 runtime improvements (cleaner follow-up, metadata defaults). Fixes: prompt cancellation restoring draft/attachments/collaboration mode, macOS app launch and Windows SQLite startup reliability, plugin manifest ordering/dedup, permission requests honoring environment identity. Current-session verification June 4, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases shows`rust-v0..` as latest stable. ↩↩

Codex CLI v0.. release notes and Codex Changelog (OpenAI Developers). Promoted to stable June 1, 2026 (17:49 UTC), following`v0..-alpha.`(May 31). Adds session archiving (`/archive` in the TUI;`codex archive`/`codex unarchive` CLI commands; archived sessions protected from resume/fork until restored); OSC 8 metadata keeping TUI markdown web links clickable with key/value fallback for cramped tables;`codex app-server --stdio` stdio-mode launch plus initial-turns-page thread resume and richer MCP server status;`CODEX_API_KEY` for remote execution on approved OpenAI hosts; short-lived server tokens for remote-control websockets;`codex sandbox setup --elevated` Windows admin provisioning (alpha); and a feature-gated standalone image-generation extension via the native image-artifact completion pipeline. Bug fixes: ChatGPT auth near-expiry token refresh with relogin on reused refresh tokens,`/diff` blocked from executing repository-provided Git helpers, sandboxed-command cleanup after interruption with deny-read rules enforced, resumed-TUI prompt-history seeding from transcripts, vim normal-mode editing, and Bedrock auth falling back to`AWS_REGION`/`AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`. Current-session verification on June 2, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases page shows`rust-v0..` as the latest stable (non-prerelease) tag. ↩↩↩↩↩

Codex Changelog (OpenAI Developers) and openai/codex releases. Codex CLI 0.. (May 26, 2026) added local conversation history search with case-insensitive content matching, made`--profile` the primary profile selector across CLI/TUI/sandbox flows with legacy-config migration guidance, improved MCP setup with per-server environment targeting plus OAuth for streamable HTTP servers, made connector tool schemas more reliable by preserving local`$ref`/`$defs` and compacting oversized schemas before exposure, enabled concurrent execution of read-only MCP tools advertising`readOnlyHint`, and added richer extension/hook context (conversation history for extension tools, persistent permission-profile metadata across auto-review runtime sync). Codex CLI 0.. (May 28, 2026) added richer`codex doctor` diagnostics covering environment, Git, terminal, app-server, and thread inventory, surfaced remote connection details and server version in`/status` when the TUI is connected over a remote, added vim mode text-object editing with improved word/line-end behavior and a configurable interrupt-turn, made`/permissions` understand and display named permission profiles, gave packaged Codex builds the bundled patched zsh helper across supported macOS and Linux, and added friendly`Sandbox` presets to the Python SDK for thread and turn APIs. Bug fixes spanned TUI markdown rendering, macOS/Zellij output stability, slash-command completion draft preservation, tmux/iTerm control-mode`Ctrl-C`,`@` app-mention filtering, and resume-flow cwd handling. ↩↩
